<script>
$(document).ready(function() {
  var headerHeight = 0;

  function dynamicHeaderHeight() {
    var $logo = $('.logo'),
        $header = $logo.closest('.header');

    $logo.on('load', function () {
      headerHeight = $header.outerHeight();
      
    }).each(function() {
      if(this.complete) {
        $(this).trigger('load');
      }
    });
  }

  var $adminBar = $('.admin-notice__bar'),
      $body = $('body.home-landing-page, body.course-landing-page, body.bundle-landing-page, body.page-template, body.coming-soon-page, body.student-certificate-page'),
      $header = $('.header'),
      $main = $('.home-landing-page main, .course-landing-page main, .bundle-landing-page main, .page-template main, .coming-soon-page main, .student-certificate-page main'),
      $dropdownToggle = $('.dropdown__toggle-button'),
      $mobileButton = $('.header__mobile-button'),
      $navigation = $('.header__nav');

  dynamicHeaderHeight();

  var adminBarHeight = $adminBar.outerHeight(),
      headerTop = $header.offset().top,
      isMobile = window.matchMedia("only screen and (max-width: 767px)");

  if(adminBarHeight != NaN) {
    $body.css({
      'margin-bottom': adminBarHeight
    });
  }

  function toggleDropdownMenu() {
    $('.dropdown').toggleClass('open');
    $dropdownToggle.attr('aria-expanded', $dropdownToggle.attr('aria-expanded') == 'true' ? 'false' : 'true');
  }
  $dropdownToggle.on('click', toggleDropdownMenu);
  $(document).on({
    keydown:function(e){
      if (e.key == "Escape" && $('.dropdown').hasClass('open')) {
        toggleDropdownMenu();
      }
    },
    click:function(e){
      if (!$(e.target).closest('.open').length && $('.dropdown').hasClass('open')) {
        toggleDropdownMenu();
      }
    }
  });
  $mobileButton.on('click', function(e) {
    $header.attr('data-menu', $header.attr('data-menu') == 'open' ? 'closed' : 'open');
    $mobileButton.attr('aria-expanded', $mobileButton.attr('aria-expanded') == 'true' ? 'false' : 'true');
    $mobileButton.toggleClass('open');
    $navigation.on('focus', false);
  });
  
    $(window).on('scroll', function (e) {
      if(!isMobile.matches) {
        if ($(window).scrollTop() > headerTop) {
          $adminBar.addClass('fixed');
          $header.addClass('fixed');
          if($header.data('menu-style') != 'floating') {
            $body.css({
              'padding-top': headerHeight,
            });
          }
        }
        if ($(window).scrollTop() <= headerTop) {
          $adminBar.removeClass('fixed');
          if($header.data('menu-style') != 'floating') {
            $body.css({
              'padding-top': 0
            });
          }
          $header.removeClass('fixed');
        }
      }
      else {
        $header.removeClass('fixed');
      }
    });
  
  $(window).on('resize', function() {
    dynamicHeaderHeight();

    

    
      if (isMobile.matches) {
        $header.removeClass('fixed');
        $body.css({
          'padding-top': 0
        });
      }
    
  });
});
</script>

<section class="curriculum">
  <div class="container">
    <div class="section-head">
      <span class="eyebrow">Self-Paced · Final Exam · ACE &amp; NCCRS Recommended</span>
      <h2>What You'll <em>Learn</em></h2>
      <p>A full semester of cybersecurity — completed at your own pace, with no deadlines or fixed class times.</p>
    </div>

    <div class="curr-layout">

      
      <div class="curr-box curr-box--outcomes">
        <div>
          <div class="curr-box-label">Learning Outcomes</div>
          <h3>What You'll Be Able to Do</h3>
        </div>
        <ul class="curr-outcomes-list">
          <li>Evaluate core cybersecurity principles and design frameworks for protecting sensitive information across industries</li>
          <li>Classify and assess different types of cyber threats and create robust security strategies for prevention and mitigation</li>
          <li>Analyze the role of human factors in cybersecurity and develop comprehensive security policies addressing error and malicious intent</li>
          <li>Utilize advanced cybersecurity tools to design practical solutions for preventing and responding to digital crimes</li>
          <li>Apply and integrate cryptographic techniques to secure information and develop countermeasures against malware and viruses</li>
          <li>Design network security systems through secure software development practices with inherent security from initial stages</li>
          <li>Examine the impact of digital communication on security and innovate measures to address platform vulnerabilities</li>
          <li>Synthesize and implement disaster recovery strategies to minimize downtime and data loss from breaches or cyberattacks</li>
        </ul>
      </div>

      
      <div class="curr-box curr-box--topics">

  if (!list) return;
  var acts = document.querySelectorAll('[data-upi-chap]');
  for (var i = 0; i < acts.length; i++) acts[i].addEventListener('click', function () {
    var open = this.getAttribute('data-upi-chap') === 'open', d = list.querySelectorAll('details');
    for (var j = 0; j < d.length; j++) d[j].open = open;
  });
})();

<section class="upi-qa" id="quick-answers" aria-labelledby="upiQaTitle">
  <div class="container">
    <div class="section-head">
      <span class="eyebrow">Quick answers</span>
      <h2 id="upiQaTitle">Cybersecurity online course: <em>quick answers</em></h2>
    </div>
    <div class="upi-qa-grid">
      <div class="upi-qa-item">
        <h3>Can I take Cybersecurity online for college credit?</h3>
        <p>Yes. Cybersecurity is a self-paced online course, and NCCRS recommends 3 semester credits for it. ACE also recommends 3 credits. A credit recommendation is what colleges review when they decide on transfer credit; the college you send your transcript to decides how many credits it accepts and where they count.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="upi-qa-item">
        <h3>Will my college accept Cybersecurity credits?</h3>
        <p>NCCRS and ACE have over 2,100 cooperating colleges that will consider accepting credits; the final decision is with the university. Look up your college in our free <a href="https://www.upistudy.com/pages/upi-s-cooperating-universities">college checker</a>, then ask your registrar how an NCCRS or ACE credit recommendation for Cybersecurity would count in your program.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="upi-qa-item">
        <h3>How much does the Cybersecurity online course cost?</h3>
        <p>$250 for this course: a one-time payment with lifetime access to it. You can also take it with the UPI Study Pro subscription (see <a href="https://www.upistudy.com/pages/pricing">pricing</a>). Textbooks, eBooks and all course materials are included at no extra fee.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="upi-qa-item">
        <h3>How long does Cybersecurity take?</h3>
        <p>Cybersecurity is self-paced: you can start any day and move at your own speed. The syllabus plans about 67 hours of study, which is about 7 weeks at 10 hours a week or 14 weeks at 5 hours a week. On average, students finish a course in 28–30 days.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="upi-qa-item">
        <h3>Are the Cybersecurity exams proctored? How is it graded?</h3>
        <p>Your grade is 25% quizzes, 25% attendance and engagement, 25% assignments and 25% the final exam; 70% is a pass. Quizzes are not proctored. The final exam is online with AI proctoring, on a desktop or laptop computer. If you do not pass the final, you get one free retake; if the retake is not passed, you restart the course.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="upi-qa-item">
        <h3>Is Cybersecurity hard? Do I need a prerequisite or a textbook?</h3>
        <p>Cybersecurity is a college-level course: NCCRS recommends it for credit in the lower division baccalaureate/associate degree category. The free syllabus lists every topic and module, so you can check the level before you enroll. No textbook to buy: all materials are included.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

  function stop(box) {
    if (box.__poster == null) return;
    box.innerHTML = box.__poster;
    box.classList.remove('is-playing');
  }
  function play(box) {
    var id = box.getAttribute('data-wistia');
    if (!id) return;
    var playing = document.querySelectorAll('.upi-tr.is-playing');
    for (var i = 0; i < playing.length; i++) if (playing[i] !== box) stop(playing[i]);
    if (box.__poster == null) box.__poster = box.innerHTML;
    var f = document.createElement('iframe');
    f.src = 'https://fast.wistia.net/embed/iframe/' + encodeURIComponent(id) +
      '?autoPlay=true&playerColor=4b8a22&endVideoBehavior=reset&playsinline=true';
    f.title = box.getAttribute('data-title') || 'Course trailer';
    f.setAttribute('allow', 'autoplay; fullscreen; picture-in-picture');
    f.setAttribute('allowfullscreen', '');
    box.innerHTML = '';
    box.appendChild(f);
    box.classList.add('is-playing');
    try { f.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) {}
    try { (window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || []).push({ event: 'course_trailer_play', trailer_course: 'Cybersecurity-ace', trailer_spot: box.getAttribute('data-spot') || '' }); } catch (e) {}
    try { if (typeof window.clarity === 'function') window.clarity('event', 'course_trailer_play'); } catch (e) {}
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var btn = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('.upi-tr-btn') : null;
    if (!btn) return;
    var box = btn.closest('.upi-tr');
    if (!box) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    play(box);
  });
})();
</script>

var SYLLABUS_URL = 'https://www.upistudy.com/syllabus/cybersecurity-ace.pdf';

function isThinkificLoggedIn() {
  
  
  return !!document.querySelector('nav.user-signed-in');
}

function handleSyllabus() {
  if (isThinkificLoggedIn()) {
    
    window.open(SYLLABUS_URL, '_blank');
  } else {
    
    openSylModal();
  }
}

 
var backdrop = document.getElementById('sylBackdrop');
var closeBtn = document.getElementById('sylClose');


var signInBtn = document.getElementById('sylSignInBtn');
var returnTo = encodeURIComponent(window.location.pathname + window.location.search);
signInBtn.href = 'https://www.upistudy.com/users/sign_in?return_to=' + returnTo;

function openSylModal() {
  backdrop.classList.add('open');
  document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
}
function closeSylModal() {
  backdrop.classList.remove('open');
  document.body.style.overflow = '';
}

closeBtn.addEventListener('click', closeSylModal);
backdrop.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
  if (e.target === backdrop) closeSylModal();
});
document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
  if (e.key === 'Escape') closeSylModal();
});

  function pickRandom(arr, n) {
    var s = arr.slice().sort(function(){ return Math.random() - .5; });
    return s.slice(0, n);
  }
  var picked = pickRandom(ALL_REVIEWS, 8);
  var track = document.getElementById('rvTrack');
  track.innerHTML = '';
  picked.forEach(function(r) {
    var card = document.createElement('div');
    card.className = 'rv-card';
    card.innerHTML =
      '<div class="rv-author" style="margin-bottom:16px;">' +
        '<div class="rv-av" style="background:' + r.color + ';">' + r.initials + '</div>' +
        '<div>' +
          '<div class="rv-name">' + r.name + '</div>' +
          '<div class="rv-role">' + r.context + '</div>' +
          '<span class="rv-tag" style="background:rgba(255,255,255,.12);color:rgba(255,255,255,.75);">' + r.tag + '</span>' +
        '</div>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<div class="rv-quote">&ldquo;' + r.quote + '&rdquo;</div>';
    track.appendChild(card);
  });
  var cards = Array.from(track.querySelectorAll('.rv-card'));
  var total = cards.length, cur = 0;
  function getVis(){ return window.innerWidth < 500 ? 1 : window.innerWidth < 860 ? 2 : 3; }
  function buildDots(){
    var el = document.getElementById('rvDots'); el.innerHTML = '';
    var pages = Math.ceil(total / getVis());
    for(var i = 0; i < pages; i++){
      var d = document.createElement('button');
      d.className = 'rv-dot' + (i === 0 ? ' on' : '');
      d.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Page ' + (i+1));
      (function(idx){ d.onclick = function(){ goTo(idx * getVis()); }; })(i);
      el.appendChild(d);
    }
  }
  function goTo(idx){
    var v = getVis(), max = Math.max(0, total - v);
    cur = Math.max(0, Math.min(idx, max));
    var cardW = cards[0].getBoundingClientRect().width;
    track.style.transform = 'translateX(-' + (cur * (cardW + 20)) + 'px)';
    document.querySelectorAll('.rv-dot').forEach(function(d, i){
      d.classList.toggle('on', i === Math.ceil(cur / v));
    });
  }
  function move(dir){
    var v = getVis();
    if(dir > 0 && cur + v >= total) goTo(0);
    else if(dir < 0 && cur <= 0) goTo(Math.max(0, total - v));
    else goTo(cur + dir * v);
  }
  document.getElementById('rvNext').onclick = function(){ move(1); };
  document.getElementById('rvPrev').onclick = function(){ move(-1); };
  buildDots();
  window.addEventListener('resize', function(){ buildDots(); goTo(cur); });
  setInterval(function(){ move(1); }, 5500);
})();
</script>

            $('#cta-overlay').removeClass('hide');
        } else {
            $('#cta-overlay').addClass('hide');
        }
      });
    }

    if(readmoreHeight > minHeight) {
      readmore.addClass('less');
      readmoreBtn.on('click', function() {
        readmore.toggleClass('less');
        if(readmore.hasClass('less')) {
          readmoreBtn.text(readMoreText);
          var sideBar = $(".sidebar-filler");
          $('html, body').animate({scrollTop: sideBar.offset().top - 0}, 'slow');
        } else {
          readmoreBtn.text(readLessText);
        }
      });
    }
    else if(readmoreHeight == minHeight) {
      readmoreBtn.addClass('hidden');
    }

      readmoreBtn.addClass('hidden');

        var showText = "Show Content";

      } else {
        var hideText = "Hide Content";
        $(this).text(hideText);
      }
    });
  });
